            raise Unprocessable("expected a JSON object with `state` and a `questions` object")
        questions = body["questions"]
        if not questions:
            raise BadRequest("no questions")
        if not all(isinstance(q, dict) for q in questions.values()):
            raise Unprocessable("every question must be an object")
        effort = body.get("effort")
        if effort is not None and not isinstance(effort, (str, dict)):
            raise Unprocessable("effort must be a tier name or an object")
        asked = effort if isinstance(effort, str) or effort is None else effort.get("tier", "fast")
        if asked is not None and asked not in EFFORT_TIERS:
            raise BadRequest(f"effort tier {asked!r} is not one of {list(EFFORT_TIERS)}")
        mode = mode or self.mode(body)
        self.check_backend()
        if mode == "bidirectional":
            self.check_two_way_flag() if self.two_way_flag else self.check_two_way()
        if self.c.images:                  # an image deployment: a request that carries an image takes the image path
            state, images = extract_images(body, self.c.max_images)
            if images:
                return self.decide_images(questions, state, images, asked, t0, mode)
        full_txt = render_state(body["state"], self.c.state_compact)
        state_txt = self.truncate_state(full_txt)
        work = []
        for qid, q in questions.items():
            text, names, descs, labels = self.c.question_prompt(state_txt, q, qid)
            work.append((qid, q.get("type", "choice"), names, descs, text, labels, noul_threshold(q)))
        full = None
        if state_txt != full_txt:          # a cut record is billed as if it had been read whole
            full = self.full_input([self.c.question_prompt(full_txt, q, qid)[0] for qid, q in questions.items()])
        shared = 0
        read = self.one if mode == "causal" else (
            (lambda text, labels: self.one(text, labels, "bidirectional")) if self.two_way_flag
            else (lambda text, labels: self.one_pooled(text, labels)[:2]))
        if len(work) == 1:
            reads = [read(work[0][4], work[0][5])]
        else:                              # concurrently: vLLM batches them and its prefix cache shares the state
            futs = [self.pool.submit(read, w[4], w[5]) for w in work]
            toks = [self.pool.submit(self.t.tokenize, w[4]) for w in work]
            reads = [f.result() for f in futs]
            # COUNT THE SHARED HEAD ONCE: the system prompt and the record are computed once by vLLM's prefix
            # cache, so N whole prompts would bill the record N times (the two-way engine has no prefix cache and
            # reads it N times: processed_input_tokens says so; input_tokens is the same count in both modes)
            shared = common_prefix_len([f.result() for f in toks])
        return self.respond(work, reads, shared, asked, t0, self.temperatures(mode), full_input=full, mode=mode)

    def full_input(self, prompts, image_tokens=0):
        """Input tokens as if the whole record had been read: the full prompts, their shared head once, plus the
        image tokens (BILLING = THE FULL INPUT: a cut record gains speed, not a lower count)."""
        ids = [self.t.tokenize(p) for p in prompts]
        return sum(len(i) for i in ids) - common_prefix_len(ids) * (len(ids) - 1) + image_tokens

    def check_chat(self):
        """Once, before the first image request: vLLM's chat template renders our messages (with no image) to exactly
        the tokens of the text prompt, so the image path differs from the trained prompt only by the image tokens."""
        if self.chat_ok:
            return
        with self.ready_lock:
            if self.chat_ok:
                return
            probe = {"type": "choice", "instructions": "Which?", "criteria": {"a": "first", "b": "second"}}
            user, _n, _d, _l = self.c.question_parts(render_state({"x": 1}, self.c.state_compact), probe)
            want = self.t.tokenize(render_chat(self.c.system, user, self.c.prefill, self.c.chat))
            got = self.t.tokenize_chat(chat_messages(self.c.system, user, self.c.prefill, []))
            if got != want:
                k = common_prefix_len([got, want]) if got and want else 0
                raise UpstreamError(f"vLLM's chat template does not render the trained prompt (tokens differ from "
                                    f"position {k} of {len(want)}); is this the model's chat_template.jinja?", 503)
            self.chat_ok = True
            sys.stderr.write("shim: image path verified (chat template renders the trained prompt)\n")

    def one_chat(self, messages, labels, attention_mode=None):
        if self.v is None:
            raise BadRequest("one-way attention is not running on this deployment")
        ids = [self.c.label_ids[l] for l in labels]
        lp, usage = self.v.label_logprobs_chat(messages, ids, self.c.max_pixels, attention_mode)
        return [lp[i] for i in ids], usage

    def temperatures(self, mode):
        """(temperature by type, image temperature, yes/no floor) of a mode."""
        if mode == "bidirectional":
            tw = self.c.two_way
            return tw.temperature_by_type, tw.image_temperature, tw.noul_floor
        return self.c.temperature_by_type, self.c.image_temperature, self.c.noul_floor

    def decide_images(self, questions, state, images, asked, t0, mode="causal"):
        """The image path: the same record and question text, the images ahead of it, read through chat completions."""
        if len(images) > self.c.max_images:
            raise Unprocessable(f"{len(images)} images; this deployment reads at most {self.c.max_images} per request")
        self.check_chat()
        full_txt = render_state(state, self.c.state_compact)
        state_txt = self.truncate_state(full_txt)
        work, msgs = [], []
        for qid, q in questions.items():
            m, text, names, descs, labels = self.c.image_prompt(state_txt, q, images, qid)
            msgs.append(m)
            work.append((qid, q.get("type", "choice"), names, descs, text, labels, noul_threshold(q)))
        shared = 0
        read = self.one_chat if mode == "causal" else (
            (lambda m_, labels: self.one_chat(m_, labels, "bidirectional")) if self.two_way_flag else self.one_chat_pooled)
        if len(work) == 1:
            reads = [read(msgs[0], work[0][5])]
        else:
            futs = [self.pool.submit(read, m, w[5]) for m, w in zip(msgs, work)]
            toks = [self.pool.submit(self.t.tokenize, w[4]) for w in work]
            reads = [f.result() for f in futs]
            toks = [f.result() for f in toks]
            # the shared head holds the images: their tokens are what vLLM counted beyond the one-slot text prompt
            extra = max(0, int(reads[0][1].get("prompt_tokens") or 0) - len(toks[0]))
            shared = common_prefix_len(toks) + extra
        full = None
        if state_txt != full_txt:          # a cut record is billed as if it had been read whole, images included
            extra = max(0, int(reads[0][1].get("prompt_tokens") or 0) - len(self.t.tokenize(work[0][4])))
            full = self.full_input([self.c.image_prompt(full_txt, q, images, qid)[1] for qid, q in questions.items()],
                                   extra)
        _t, image_t, floor = self.temperatures(mode)
        return self.respond(work, reads, shared, asked, t0, (image_t, None, floor), len(images), full, mode)

    def respond(self, work, reads, shared, asked, t0, temperature, n_images=0, full_input=None, mode="causal"):
        """The response: each answer at `temperature` (one value, {type: T}, or the (by type, image, floor) triple of
        a mode; then the yes/no floor), and usage with the shared head once."""
        floor = self.c.noul_floor
        if isinstance(temperature, tuple):
            temperature, _it, floor = temperature
        answers, ntok, processed = {}, 0, 0
        for (qid, typ, names, descs, _t, _l, thr), (lp, usage) in zip(work, reads):
            t = temperature[typ] if isinstance(temperature, dict) else temperature
            answers[qid] = format_answer(typ, names, descs, lp, t, floor, thr)
            ntok += int(usage.get("prompt_tokens") or 0)
        processed = ntok                   # what the engine read (the two-way engine reads every prompt whole)
        ntok -= shared * (len(work) - 1)   # = the shared head + each question's tail
        used = {"tier": "fast", "passes_per_decision": 1}
        if asked and asked != "fast":
            used["requested"] = asked
            used["note"] = (f"tier {asked!r} is accepted for API compatibility but not implemented; this response "
                            f"was produced at tier 'fast' with one forward pass per decision")
        out = {"model": self.c.model, "answers": answers, "probability_source": "native", "effort_used": used,
               # input_tokens: the full input (processed_input_tokens: what vLLM read, less when a long record was
               # cut); output_tokens: one label token read per answered question
               "usage": {"input_tokens": ntok if full_input is None else full_input,
                         "processed_input_tokens": processed if mode == "bidirectional" else ntok,
                         "output_tokens": len(work), "latency_ms": round((time.time() - t0) * 1000, 1)}}
        if n_images:
            out["usage"]["images"] = n_images
        out["attention_mode"] = mode       # always: the mode the answers were read in
        return out

    def health(self):
        c = self.c
        return {"ok": bool(self.ready), "model": c.model, "shim": SHIM_VERSION,
                "backend": f"vllm {self.v.base}" if self.v is not None else None,
                "layout": c.layout, "chat_template": c.chat, "state_compact": c.state_compact, "prefill": c.prefill,
                "max_state_tokens": c.max_state_tokens,
                "noul_floor": {"value": c.noul_floor, "source": c.noul_floor_source},
                "temperature_by_type": dict(c.temperature_by_type),
                "temperature_source": c.temperature_source, "max_options": len(c.labels),
                "vllm_max_model_len": self.max_model_len,
                **({"images": {"max_images": c.max_images, "max_pixels": c.max_pixels or None,
                               "temperature": c.image_temperature, "verified": bool(self.chat_ok)}}
                   if c.images else {}),
                "attention_mode": self.default_mode(),
                "modes": ([m for m, e in (("causal", self.v), ("bidirectional", self.p)) if e is not None]
                          + (["bidirectional"] if self.two_way_flag and self.p is None else [])),
                **({"two_way": {**c.two_way.describe(), "backend": f"vllm {self.p.base}", "verified": bool(self.two_way_ok),
                                "probe_anchor_distance": self.two_way_anchor, "probe_separation": self.two_way_separation}}
                   if self.p is not None else {})}


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- OpenAI Decisions
# POST /v1/decisions speaks OpenAI's Decisions API (client.decisions.create in the `openai` Python package): `input` (a
# string, or user messages with input_text / input_image parts) and an ordered `questions` list of predicate / choice
# / score questions. Each request is mapped onto the /v1/systemone decision above (the same readout and calibration),
# in the option format the model was trained on: a choice's string value is the option's name and its description the
# option's text, a score level is its label (with ": description" when given), a predicate is a yes/no question with
# the default wording. The answers come back in OpenAI's shape. `model` must be the served model (or its name without
# the organisation prefix, e.g. "h2o-lightning-4b"). An image's `detail` is accepted and not used (the deployment's own
# image cap applies).
def _oai_fail(text):
    raise BadRequest(text)


def _oai_fields(value, allowed, required=(), path="body"):
    if not isinstance(value, dict):
        _oai_fail(path + " must be an object")
    if set(value) - set(allowed):
        _oai_fail(path + " unknown fields: " + ", ".join(sorted(set(value) - set(allowed))))
    if set(required) - set(value):
        _oai_fail(path + " missing fields: " + ", ".join(sorted(set(required) - set(value))))


def _oai_string(value, path):
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        _oai_fail(path + " must be a string")


def openai_request(body, served):
    """(a /v1/systemone body, the answer mapping) for an OpenAI Decisions request; BadRequest when it is not one."""
    _oai_fields(body, {"model", "input", "questions", "safety_identifier", "attention_mode"}, ("model", "input", "questions"))
    _oai_string(body["model"], "model")
    if body["model"] not in (served, served.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]):
        _oai_fail(f"model must be the served model {served!r}")
    if body.get("safety_identifier") is not None:
        _oai_string(body["safety_identifier"], "safety_identifier")
    inp, images = body["input"], []
    if isinstance(inp, str):
        state = inp
    elif isinstance(inp, list) and inp:
        parts = []
        for message in inp:
            _oai_fields(message, {"role", "type", "content"}, ("role", "content"), "input message")
            if message["role"] != "user" or message.get("type", "message") != "message":
                _oai_fail("only user messages are supported")
            content = message["content"]
            if isinstance(content, str):
                parts.append({"role": "user", "content": [{"type": "input_text", "text": content}]})
                continue
            if not isinstance(content, list):
                _oai_fail("message content must be a string or ordered parts")
            ordered = []
            for part in content:
                if not isinstance(part, dict):
                    _oai_fail("an input part must be an object")
                if part.get("type") == "input_text":
                    _oai_fields(part, {"type", "text"}, ("type", "text"), "input_text")
                    _oai_string(part["text"], "input_text.text")
                    ordered.append(dict(part))
                elif part.get("type") == "input_image":
                    _oai_fields(part, {"type", "image_url", "detail"}, ("type", "image_url"), "input_image")
                    _oai_string(part["image_url"], "image_url")
                    url = part["image_url"]   # a data URI, or an http(s) link fetched by the safe fetch above
                    if not url.startswith("data:image/") and not (
                            url.lower().startswith(("https://", "http://"))
                            and os.environ.get("LIGHTNING_IMAGE_URLS", "fetch").lower() != "off"):
                        _oai_fail("image_url must be a data:image/... URI or an https image link")
                    if part.get("detail", "auto") not in ("auto", "low", "high", "original", None):
                        _oai_fail("image detail must be auto, low, high or original")
                    images.append(url)
                    ordered.append({"type": "input_image", "image_index": len(images) - 1})
                else:
                    _oai_fail("unsupported input part; only input_text and input_image are supported")
            parts.append({"role": "user", "content": ordered})
        state = {"messages": parts}
    else:
        _oai_fail("input must be a string or a non-empty array of user messages")
    if not isinstance(body["questions"], list) or not body["questions"]:
        _oai_fail("questions must be a non-empty ordered array")
    questions, mapping = {}, []
    for index, q in enumerate(body["questions"]):
        if not isinstance(q, dict):
            _oai_fail("a question must be an object")
        typ = q.get("type")
        _oai_fields(q, {"type", "instructions", "name"} | ({"choices"} if typ == "choice" else {"levels"}
                                                          if typ == "score" else set()), ("type", "instructions"),
                    "question")
        _oai_string(q["instructions"], "question.instructions")
        if "name" in q:
            _oai_string(q["name"], "question.name")
        internal, entry = {"instructions": q["instructions"]}, {"type": typ, "name": q.get("name")}
        if typ == "predicate":
            internal["type"] = "noul"
        elif typ == "choice":
            choices = q.get("choices")
            if not isinstance(choices, list) or not 2 <= len(choices) <= 255:
                _oai_fail("choices must contain 2..255 unique typed options")
            seen, values, descs = set(), [], []
            for number, choice in enumerate(choices):
                _oai_fields(choice, {"value", "description"}, ("value",), "choice")
                value = choice["value"]
                if type(value) not in (str, bool):
                    _oai_fail("a choice value must be a string or a boolean")
                if (type(value).__name__, value) in seen:
                    _oai_fail("choice values must be unique by type and value")
                seen.add((type(value).__name__, value))
                if "description" in choice:
                    _oai_string(choice["description"], "choice.description")
                values.append(value)
                descs.append(choice.get("description") or None)
            # the option names the model reads: the string values themselves (a boolean as true / false); option_N
            # for every option when two would read the same
            names = [v if isinstance(v, str) else ("true" if v else "false") for v in values]
            if len(set(names)) < len(names) or not all(n.strip() for n in names):
                names = ["option_" + str(j) for j in range(len(values))]
                descs = [d if d is not None else json.dumps(v) for d, v in zip(descs, values)]
            internal.update(type="choice", criteria=dict(zip(names, descs)))
            entry["values"], entry["names"] = values, names
        elif typ == "score":
            levels = q.get("levels")
            if not isinstance(levels, list) or not 1 <= len(levels) <= 255:
                _oai_fail("levels must contain 1..255 ordered levels")
            for level in levels:
                _oai_fields(level, {"label", "description"}, ("label",), "level")
                _oai_string(level["label"], "level.label")
                if "description" in level:
                    _oai_string(level["description"], "level.description")
            internal.update(type="score", criteria=[x["label"] + (": " + x["description"] if x.get("description") else "")
                                                    for x in levels])
            entry["labels"] = [x["label"] for x in levels]
        else:
            _oai_fail("question type must be predicate, choice or score")
        questions["q_" + str(index)] = internal
        mapping.append(entry)
    return {"state": state, "questions": questions, **({"images": images} if images else {}),
            **({"attention_mode": body["attention_mode"]} if body.get("attention_mode") is not None else {})}, mapping


def openai_response(core, mapping):
    """A /v1/systemone response in OpenAI's Decisions shape."""
    answers = []
    for i, meta in enumerate(mapping):
        a = core["answers"]["q_" + str(i)]
        common = {"type": meta["type"], "name": meta["name"]}
        if meta["type"] == "predicate":
            answers.append({**common, "probability": a["noul"]})
        elif meta["type"] == "choice":
            p = a["probabilities"]
            answers.append({**common, "choice": meta["values"][meta["names"].index(a["choice"])],
                            "confidence": a["confidence"],
                            "probabilities": [{"value": v, "probability": p[n]}
                                              for v, n in zip(meta["values"], meta["names"])]})
        else:
            answers.append({**common, "score": a["score"], "confidence": a["confidence"],
                            "probabilities": [{"label": label, "value": j, "probability": a["probabilities"][str(j)]}
                                              for j, label in enumerate(meta["labels"])]})
    usage = core["usage"]
    return {"model": core["model"], "answers": answers,
            "usage": {"input_tokens": usage["input_tokens"], "output_tokens": usage["output_tokens"],
                      "total_tokens": usage["input_tokens"] + usage["output_tokens"],
                      "input_tokens_details": {"cached_tokens": 0, "cache_write_tokens": 0},
                      "processed_input_tokens": usage["processed_input_tokens"],
                      "output_tokens_details": {"reasoning_tokens": 0}}}


def make_handler(shim):
    class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
        protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"
        # TCP_NODELAY on each client connection: the headers and the body go out as two writes, and on a kept-alive
        # connection Nagle would hold the body until the client's (delayed, ~40 ms) ACK of the headers
        disable_nagle_algorithm = True

        def log_message(self, fmt, *args):
            if os.environ.get("SHIM_ACCESS_LOG", "") == "1":
                sys.stderr.write("shim %s\n" % (fmt % args))

        def _send(self, code, obj):
            payload = json.dumps(obj).encode()
            self.send_response(code)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(payload)))
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(payload)

        def do_GET(self):
            if self.path.startswith("/health"):
                try:
                    shim.check_backend()
                except (UpstreamError, Unprocessable) as e:
                    return self._send(503, {**shim.health(), "error": str(e)})
                # SHIM_READY_FILE (set by serve.sh): not ready until that file exists, i.e. until the launcher's warm-up
                # has passed; "/health?live=1" answers meanwhile (the launcher's own check)
                flag = os.environ.get("SHIM_READY_FILE", "").strip()
                live = urllib.parse.parse_qs(urllib.parse.urlsplit(self.path).query).get("live") == ["1"]
                if flag and not os.path.exists(flag) and not live:
                    return self._send(503, {**shim.health(), "ok": False, "warming": True})
                return self._send(200, shim.health())
            if self.path.startswith("/v1/models"):
                return self._send(200, {"object": "list", "data": [{"id": shim.c.model, "readout": "native label "
                                                                    "log-probabilities over vLLM"}]})
            self._send(404, {"error": "not found"})

        def do_POST(self):
            openai = self.path.startswith("/v1/decisions")
            if not openai and not self.path.startswith(("/v1/systemone", "/api/alpha/decisions")):
                return self._send(404, {"error": "not found"})
            ctype = self.headers.get("Content-Type") or ""
            try:
                n = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length") or 0)
                raw = self.rfile.read(n)
                if shim.c.images and ctype.lower().startswith("multipart/form-data"):
                    body = parse_multipart(ctype, raw)
                else:
                    body = json.loads(raw or b"null")
            except Unprocessable as e:
                return self._send(422, {"detail": str(e)})
            except (ValueError, OSError) as e:
                return self._send(422, {"detail": f"request body is not JSON: {e}"})
            try:
                if openai:
                    request, mapping = openai_request(body, shim.c.model)
                    return self._send(200, openai_response(shim.decide(request), mapping))
                return self._send(200, shim.decide(body))
            except BadRequest as e:
                return self._send(400, {"detail": str(e)})
            except Unprocessable as e:
                return self._send(422, {"detail": str(e)})
            except UpstreamError as e:
                return self._send(e.status, {"detail": str(e)})
            except Exception as e:   # one item's failure is a 422, never a 500
                import traceback
                traceback.print_exc()
                return self._send(422, {"detail": f"{type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:300]}"})

    return Handler


class Server(ThreadingHTTPServer):
    daemon_threads = True
    request_queue_size = 512      # the standard library's listen backlog of 5 resets concurrent clients


def main(argv=None):
    here = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.split("\n")[0])
    ap.add_argument("--config", default=os.environ.get("SHIM_CONFIG", os.path.join(here, "serve_config.json")))
    ap.add_argument("--vllm", default=os.environ.get("SHIM_VLLM") or None,
                    help="the generate-mode vLLM (one-way readouts, Box); default http://127.0.0.1:8000 unless only --vllm-two-way is given")
    ap.add_argument("--vllm-two-way", default=os.environ.get("SHIM_VLLM_TWO_WAY") or None,
                    help="the pooling-runner vLLM (is_causal false) that serves two-way attention")
    ap.add_argument("--model-dir", default=os.environ.get("SHIM_MODEL_DIR") or None,
                    help="the served weights' folder (model.safetensors), for the label rows' identity check; default: the config's folder")
    ap.add_argument("--model", default=os.environ.get("SHIM_MODEL"),
                    help="vLLM's --served-model-name (default: serve_config.json's `model`)")
    ap.add_argument("--host", default=os.environ.get("SHIM_HOST", "127.0.0.1"))
    ap.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("SHIM_PORT", "8741")))
    ap.add_argument("--min-context", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("SHIM_MIN_CONTEXT", "4096")))
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    cfg = json.load(open(a.config))
    cfg.setdefault("_config_dir", os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(a.config)))   # two_way.label_rows is relative to it
    if a.model:
        cfg["model"] = a.model
    contract = Contract(cfg)
    if a.vllm is None and a.vllm_two_way is None:
        a.vllm = "http://127.0.0.1:8000"
    one = VLLM(a.vllm, contract.model) if a.vllm else None
    two = VLLM(a.vllm_two_way, contract.model) if a.vllm_two_way else None
    shim = Shim(contract, one, a.min_context, two, a.model_dir or cfg["_config_dir"])
    srv = Server((a.host, a.port), make_handler(shim))
    print(f"shim on {a.host}:{a.port} (model {contract.model}, {len(contract.labels)} labels; default mode {shim.default_mode()}"
          + (f"; one-way -> {a.vllm}, T {contract.temperature_by_type}, yes/no floor {contract.noul_floor}" if one else "")
          + (f"; two-way -> {a.vllm_two_way}, T {contract.two_way.temperature_by_type}, yes/no floor "
             f"{contract.two_way.noul_floor}" if two else "")
          + ")", flush=True)
    srv.serve_forever()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: pooled_readout.py
================================================
"""The pooled readout: label log-probabilities from the pooling runner's final hidden state (two-way attention).

Two-way (bidirectional) reading runs vLLM's POOLING runner, whose embeddings endpoint returns the final hidden
state at the last prompt position (after the final norm, before lm_head; `--pooler-config '{"task": "embed",
"seq_pooling_type": "LAST", "use_activation": false}'`) and nothing else: no logits, no log-probabilities. The shim does the lm_head's last
step itself, for the label tokens only:

    logit[id]    = h . lm_head[id]                     (softcapped when the model has a final logit softcap)
    log P(label) = logsumexp(logit over the label's ids) - logsumexp(logit over every asked label's ids)

That is the softmax restricted to the asked labels' tokens, which is what the one-way path reads from vLLM's
`logprob_token_ids` too (the full-vocabulary normaliser cancels). The lm_head rows come from the LABEL-ROW FILE
shipped beside serve_config.json (`label_rows.lightning`: the safetensors FORMAT under a name vLLM's weight loader
does not glob, since `*.safetensors` beside model.safetensors would be read as a weight shard): the fp32 tensor `W`
[n_tokens, hidden] and `token_ids` int64 [n_tokens] (the flattened label groups in the contract's label order), and
in the metadata the hidden size, the softcap, the label order, the groups, the rows' sha256 and the weights' sha256.
This module reads and writes that format itself (8-byte little-endian header length, JSON header, raw bytes) so the
shim needs neither torch nor safetensors; numpy is used when present.
"""
import base64
import json
import math
import struct

try:
    import numpy as np
except ImportError:  # optional: the pure-Python path is used without it
    np = None

FORMAT = "lightning-label-rows-1"
FLOOR = 1e-12


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- label rows
class LabelRows:
    """The exported lm_head rows: `ids` (token ids, the row order), `rows` (n x hidden, fp32), `groups`
    [(label, [ids]), ...] in the trained label order, `softcap` (0 = none), `hidden`, and the file's metadata."""

    def __init__(self, ids, rows, groups, softcap, metadata):
        self.ids = [int(i) for i in ids]
        self.rows = rows
        self.groups = [(str(label), [int(i) for i in g]) for label, g in groups]
        self.softcap = float(softcap or 0.0)
        self.metadata = dict(metadata)
        self.hidden = int(metadata["hidden"])
        self.index = {i: k for k, i in enumerate(self.ids)}
        self.labels = [label for label, _ in self.groups]
        if len(self.index) != len(self.ids):
            raise ValueError("label rows: a token id appears twice")
        missing = [i for _, g in self.groups for i in g if i not in self.index]
        if missing:
            raise ValueError(f"label rows: no row for token ids {missing[:5]} of the label groups")
        seen = [i for _, g in self.groups for i in g]
        if len(set(seen)) != len(seen):
            raise ValueError("label rows: a token id belongs to two labels")
        if len(set(self.labels)) != len(self.labels):
            raise ValueError("label rows: the labels are not distinct")

    def group_for(self, label):
        return next(g for l, g in self.groups if l == label)


def _header(ids, hidden, groups, softcap, meta):
    m = {
        "format": FORMAT,
        "hidden": str(int(hidden)),
        "softcap": "none" if not softcap else repr(float(softcap)),
        "label_order": json.dumps([str(l) for l, _ in groups], ensure_ascii=False),
        "groups": json.dumps([[str(l), [int(i) for i in g]] for l, g in groups], ensure_ascii=False),
    }
    for k, v in (meta or {}).items():
        if v is not None:
            m[str(k)] = v if isinstance(v, str) else json.dumps(v)
    return m


def rows_sha256(rows):
    """sha256 of the fp32 rows as written (little-endian, row by row): the file's `lm_head_sha256`."""
    import hashlib
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    for r in rows:
        h.update(struct.pack(f"<{len(r)}f", *[float(x) for x in r]))
    return h.hexdigest()


def write_label_rows(path, rows, ids, groups, softcap, hidden=None, meta=None):
    """Write the label-row file: tensors `W` fp32 [n, hidden] (rows in the order of `ids`) and `token_ids` int64
    [n]; metadata as _header plus `lm_head_sha256` of the rows (and whatever `meta` adds)."""
    rows = [[float(x) for x in r] for r in rows]
    if len(rows) != len(ids):
        raise ValueError(f"label rows: {len(rows)} rows for {len(ids)} token ids")
    hidden = int(hidden if hidden is not None else (len(rows[0]) if rows else 0))
    if any(len(r) != hidden for r in rows):
        raise ValueError("label rows: the rows do not all have the hidden size")
    LabelRows(ids, rows, groups, softcap, {"hidden": hidden})  # the same checks the reader makes
    n_w = len(rows) * hidden * 4
    n_i = len(ids) * 8
    header = {
        "__metadata__": {**_header(ids, hidden, groups, softcap, meta), "lm_head_sha256": rows_sha256(rows)},
        "W": {"dtype": "F32", "shape": [len(rows), hidden], "data_offsets": [0, n_w]},
        "token_ids": {"dtype": "I64", "shape": [len(ids)], "data_offsets": [n_w, n_w + n_i]},
    }
    hb = json.dumps(header, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")
    hb += b" " * (-len(hb) % 8)  # safetensors pads the header to 8 bytes
    with open(path, "wb") as f:
        f.write(struct.pack("<Q", len(hb)))
        f.write(hb)
        for r in rows:
            f.write(struct.pack(f"<{hidden}f", *r))
        f.write(struct.pack(f"<{len(ids)}q", *[int(i) for i in ids]))


def read_label_rows(path):
    """Read the label-row file written by write_label_rows (or by safetensors with the same tensors and metadata)."""
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        (n,) = struct.unpack("<Q", f.read(8))
        header = json.loads(f.read(n).decode("utf-8"))
        meta = header.get("__metadata__") or {}
        if meta.get("format") != FORMAT:
            raise ValueError(f"label rows: {path} is not a {FORMAT} file (format {meta.get('format')!r})")
        w, t = header.get("W"), header.get("token_ids")
        if not isinstance(w, dict) or w.get("dtype") != "F32" or len(w.get("shape") or []) != 2:
            raise ValueError(f"label rows: no fp32 2-d tensor 'W' in {path}")
        if not isinstance(t, dict) or t.get("dtype") != "I64" or len(t.get("shape") or []) != 1:
            raise ValueError(f"label rows: no int64 1-d tensor 'token_ids' in {path}")
        rows_n, hidden = (int(x) for x in w["shape"])
        a, b = (int(x) for x in w["data_offsets"])
        c, d = (int(x) for x in t["data_offsets"])
        if b - a != rows_n * hidden * 4 or d - c != int(t["shape"][0]) * 8 or int(t["shape"][0]) != rows_n:
            raise ValueError("label rows: the tensors' byte ranges do not match their shapes")
        f.seek(8 + n + a)
        raw = f.read(b - a)
        f.seek(8 + n + c)
        raw_ids = f.read(d - c)
    if len(raw) != b - a or len(raw_ids) != d - c:
        raise ValueError("label rows: the file is truncated")
    ids = list(struct.unpack(f"<{rows_n}q", raw_ids))
    if np is not None:
        rows = np.frombuffer(raw, dtype="<f4").reshape(rows_n, hidden).astype(np.float64)
    else:
        flat = struct.unpack(f"<{rows_n * hidden}f", raw)
        rows = [list(flat[k * hidden : (k + 1) * hidden]) for k in range(rows_n)]
    if int(meta["hidden"]) != hidden:
        raise ValueError("label rows: the hidden metadata does not match the tensor")
    sc = meta.get("softcap", "none")
    softcap = 0.0 if sc in ("none", "", None) else float(sc)
    if "groups" in meta:
        groups = json.loads(meta["groups"])
    else:  # one token per label, in label order
        labels = json.loads(meta["label_order"])
        if len(labels) != rows_n:
            raise ValueError("label rows: no groups metadata and the label order does not match the ids one to one")
        groups = [[l, [i]] for l, i in zip(labels, ids)]
    return LabelRows(ids, rows, groups, softcap, meta)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ readout
def softcap(x, cap):
    """A final logit softcap (some models have one); identity for cap 0 / None."""
    return cap * math.tanh(x / cap) if cap else x


def decode_pooled(data, hidden=None):
    """The pooled vector from an embeddings / pooling response item: a float list, or base64 fp32 little-endian
    (encoding_format base64, embed_dtype float32). Checks the length against `hidden` when given."""
    if isinstance(data, str):
        raw = base64.b64decode(data, validate=True)
        if len(raw) % 4:
            raise ValueError("pooled vector: the base64 payload is not whole fp32 values")
        h = (
            np.frombuffer(raw, dtype="<f4").astype(np.float64)
            if np is not None
            else list(struct.unpack(f"<{len(raw) // 4}f", raw))
        )
    elif isinstance(data, list) and all(isinstance(x, (int, float)) and not isinstance(x, bool) for x in data):
        h = np.asarray(data, dtype=np.float64) if np is not None else [float(x) for x in data]
    else:
        raise ValueError("pooled vector: expected a float list or a base64 string")
    if hidden is not None and len(h) != hidden:
        raise ValueError(f"pooled vector has {len(h)} values; the label rows have hidden size {hidden}")
    if not all(math.isfinite(float(x)) for x in h):
        raise ValueError("pooled vector holds a non-finite value")
    return h


def l2_norm(h):
    return math.sqrt(sum(float(x) * float(x) for x in h))


def check_not_normalised(h, tol=1e-3):
    """An engine started with the activation on returns unit vectors and the logits are garbage: refuse."""
    if abs(l2_norm(h) - 1.0) < tol:
        raise ValueError("the pooled vector is L2-normalised (norm 1.0): the engine's pooler runs with "
                         "use_activation on; serve it with use_activation false")


def label_logits(h, rows, ids=None):
    """{token id: softcapped logit} for the label rows' ids (or the subset `ids`) at the pooled state h."""
    want = rows.ids if ids is None else [int(i) for i in ids]
    k = [rows.index[i] for i in want]
    if np is not None:
        hv = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
        raw = (np.asarray(rows.rows)[k] @ hv).tolist()
    else:
        raw = [sum(a * b for a, b in zip(rows.rows[j], h)) for j in k]
    return {i: softcap(float(x), rows.softcap) for i, x in zip(want, raw)}


def _logsumexp(v):
    m = max(v)
    return m + math.log(sum(math.exp(x - m) for x in v))


def group_logprobs(logits, groups):
    """log P(label) for the labels in `groups` [[label, [ids]], ...] from {id: logit}: per-label logsumexp over the
    label's ids, renormalised over these labels (the softmax restricted to their tokens), floored at FLOOR."""
    acc = []
    for _, ids in groups:
        v = [logits[i] for i in ids]
        if not v or not all(isinstance(x, (int, float)) and not isinstance(x, bool) and math.isfinite(x) for x in v):
            raise ValueError(f"an invalid logit among token ids {list(ids)[:5]}")
        acc.append(_logsumexp(v))
    z = _logsumexp(acc)
    return [max(a - z, math.log(FLOOR)) for a in acc]


def read_pooled(data, rows, groups):
    """One question's readout: (log P(label) in the order of `groups`, the pooled vector) from a response item's
    vector (`embedding` of /v1/embeddings, `data` of /pooling)."""
    h = decode_pooled(data, rows.hidden)
    ids = [i for _, g in groups for i in g]
    return group_logprobs(label_logits(h, rows, ids), groups), h


def probabilities(logprobs, temperature=1.0):
    """softmax(log-probabilities / T): the distribution the mode probe compares (T = 1)."""
    v = [x / float(temperature) for x in logprobs]
    m = max(v)
    e = [math.exp(x - m) for x in v]
    s = sum(e)
    return [x / s for x in e]

    "merge_size": 2,
    "image_mean": [
        0.5,
        0.5,
        0.5
    ],
    "image_std": [
        0.5,
        0.5,
        0.5
    ],
    "processor_class": "Qwen3VLProcessor",
    "image_processor_type": "Qwen2VLImageProcessorFast"
}

FILE: serve.sh
================================================
#!/bin/bash
# H2O-Lightning-4B: one vLLM engine + the shim, on one GPU. Run it from a download of the model repo:
#     hf download h2oai/h2o-lightning-4b --local-dir h2o-lightning-4b && bash h2o-lightning-4b/serve.sh
# Then POST decisions to http://127.0.0.1:${PORT:-8741}/v1/systemone. Ctrl-C stops both.
# MODEL defaults to the directory this script is in (the downloaded weights); VLLM_PORT, PORT, MAX_LEN, GPU_UTIL override.
# A model folder with a vision tower (config.json has "vision_config") also gets vLLM's image limits: MAX_IMAGES images
# per prompt (default 4), no video, and images scaled to at most MAX_PIXELS pixels (default: serve_config.json's
# image.max_pixels, the cap the shim sends with each request; 1605632 when the config sets none).
# Before it reports ready (the ready line, and /health 200), it warms every path the card uses (see warm() below).
# VLLM_ARGS adds flags to `vllm serve` (space-separated; values cannot contain spaces), for example the
# high-concurrency ones in the card's "Serving at scale".
# VLLM_PATCHES=1 first applies the two vLLM 0.30.0 source patches in vllm_patches/ to the vLLM that runs here (see the
# card): it edits that environment's vLLM install, so use a dedicated virtual environment. Default off (stock vLLM).
# ATTENTION MODE. TWO_WAY=1 (the default; also ATTENTION=two-way) serves two-way (bidirectional) attention, the
# recommended mode, on ONE generate-mode engine of the vLLM build with per-request attention (github.com/h2oai/vllm,
# commit 6bc6d5a839923abd64ab1f63a513258d74a5e16f): the shim sends vllm_xargs attention_mode=bidirectional per request,
# and the same engine also answers one-way requests ("attention_mode": "causal") and Box questions. The engine runs with
# --attention-backend TRITON_ATTN and --max-num-batched-tokens = MAX_LEN (a two-way prompt is prefilled whole); the
# shim checks the engine's two-way reads against fixed probes before its first two-way answer. TWO_WAY=0 (or
# ATTENTION=one-way) is the fallback: one stock vLLM 0.30.0 in generate mode, one-way only. The earlier two-engine
# two-way setup (a pooling-runner engine) is no longer supported: TWO_WAY=1 with ONE_ENGINE=0 stops with a message.
set -euo pipefail
HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
MODEL="${MODEL:-$HERE}"; VLLM_PORT="${VLLM_PORT:-8000}"; PORT="${PORT:-8741}"
case "${ATTENTION:-}" in "") ;; one-way|causal) TWO_WAY=0;; two-way|bidirectional) TWO_WAY=1;; *) echo "ATTENTION must be one-way or two-way"; exit 1;; esac
TWO_WAY="${TWO_WAY:-1}"
case "$TWO_WAY" in 0|1) ;; *) echo "TWO_WAY must be 0 or 1"; exit 1;; esac
# two-way runs on one engine only; ONE_ENGINE defaults to TWO_WAY (ONE_ENGINE=1 with TWO_WAY=0 also works: the build's
# engine answering one-way by default)
case "${ONE_ENGINE:-$TWO_WAY}" in 0|1) ;; *) echo "ONE_ENGINE must be 0 or 1"; exit 1;; esac
ONE_ENGINE="${ONE_ENGINE:-$TWO_WAY}"
if [ "$TWO_WAY" = 1 ] && [ "$ONE_ENGINE" = 0 ]; then
  echo "TWO_WAY=1 needs ONE_ENGINE=1 (the default): the two-engine two-way setup (a pooling-runner engine) is no longer supported. Two-way runs on one engine of the h2oai/vllm build (see the model card); TWO_WAY=0 serves one-way on stock vLLM"; exit 1
fi
# a folder from the first v1.3 download still holds label_rows.safetensors, which vLLM reads as a weight shard and
# fails on ("no module or parameter named 'W'"): refuse in every mode until it is deleted
[ -e "$MODEL/label_rows.safetensors" ] && { echo "delete $MODEL/label_rows.safetensors (an older download; vLLM reads every *.safetensors as weights); the label rows now ship as label_rows.lightning"; exit 1; }
case "${LIGHTNING_BOX_ENABLED:-0}" in 1|true|yes|on) BOX=1;; *) BOX=0;; esac
# the one engine needs the vLLM build with per-request attention: check the vLLM this script will start before it starts
if [ "$ONE_ENGINE" = 1 ]; then
  VPY="$(dirname "$(command -v vllm 2>/dev/null || echo /nonexistent/vllm)")/python3"; [ -x "$VPY" ] || VPY=python3
  "$VPY" -c "from vllm.v1.request import parse_attention_mode" 2>/dev/null || { echo "ONE_ENGINE=1 (two-way, the default) needs the vLLM build with per-request attention (github.com/h2oai/vllm @6bc6d5a839923abd64ab1f63a513258d74a5e16f; see the model card); this vLLM is a stock build. TWO_WAY=0 serves one-way on stock vLLM"; exit 1; }
  # every file of the build must match engine/vllm_per_request_attention.SHA256SUMS (checked on every start)
  SUMS="$HERE/engine/vllm_per_request_attention.SHA256SUMS"
  [ -f "$SUMS" ] || { echo "ONE_ENGINE=1 needs engine/vllm_per_request_attention.SHA256SUMS beside serve.sh (shipped with the model)"; exit 1; }
  SITE="$("$VPY" -c 'import os, vllm; print(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(vllm.__file__)))' 2>/dev/null)" || SITE=""
  [ -n "$SITE" ] && (cd "$SITE" && sha256sum -c --quiet "$SUMS" > /dev/null 2>&1) || { echo "ONE_ENGINE=1: the per-request attention build in ${SITE:-this vLLM} does not match engine/vllm_per_request_attention.SHA256SUMS (h2oai/vllm @6bc6d5a839923abd64ab1f63a513258d74a5e16f); reinstall it as the model card shows. TWO_WAY=0 serves one-way on stock vLLM"; exit 1; }
fi
# VLLM_PATCHES=1: vLLM must be 0.30.0; each patch's sha256 must match vllm_patches/SHA256SUMS and it must apply exactly
# (no fuzz); a patch already in place is left as it is; one that neither is in place nor applies stops the script.
# Every file a patch touches becomes a private copy first: uv virtual environments hard-link installed files to a
# shared cache, and patching through the link would change every environment linked to it.
case "${VLLM_PATCHES:-0}" in 0|1) ;; *) echo "VLLM_PATCHES must be 0 or 1"; exit 1;; esac
if [ "${VLLM_PATCHES:-0}" = 1 ] && [ "$ONE_ENGINE" = 1 ]; then
  echo "VLLM_PATCHES=1 is for stock vLLM (one-way, TWO_WAY=0): the per-request attention build changes the same files, and its sha256 check would refuse every later start"; exit 1
fi
if [ "${VLLM_PATCHES:-0}" = 1 ]; then
  command -v patch > /dev/null || { echo "VLLM_PATCHES=1 needs the patch program (for example: apt-get install patch)"; exit 1; }
  SP="$(python3 -c 'import importlib.util as u, importlib.metadata as m, os, sys; v = m.version("vllm"); sys.exit("VLLM_PATCHES=1: vLLM " + v + " found; the patches are for 0.30.0") if not v.startswith("0.30.0") else print(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(u.find_spec("vllm").origin)))')"
  (cd "$HERE/vllm_patches" && sha256sum -c --quiet SHA256SUMS) || { echo "vllm_patches/: sha256 mismatch"; exit 1; }
  for P in "$HERE"/vllm_patches/*.patch; do
    if patch -p1 -R -s -f -F0 --dry-run -d "$SP" < "$P" > /dev/null 2>&1; then
      echo "vLLM patch $(basename "$P"): already in place"
    elif patch -p1 -N -s -f -F0 --dry-run -d "$SP" < "$P" > /dev/null 2>&1; then
      for T in $(grep '^+++ b/' "$P" | sed 's#^+++ b/##'); do
        if [ -e "$SP/$T" ] && [ "$(stat -c %h "$SP/$T")" -gt 1 ]; then
          cp --remove-destination -p "$SP/$T" "$SP/$T.tmp.$$" && mv -f "$SP/$T.tmp.$$" "$SP/$T" \
            || { echo "vLLM patch: cannot make $T a private copy"; exit 1; }
        fi
      done
      patch -p1 -N -s -f -F0 -d "$SP" < "$P" > /dev/null || { echo "vLLM patch $(basename "$P"): apply failed"; exit 1; }
      find "$SP/vllm" -name __pycache__ -prune -exec rm -rf {} + 2> /dev/null || true
      echo "vLLM patch $(basename "$P"): applied"
    else
      echo "vLLM patch $(basename "$P"): does not apply to the vLLM in $SP (it is for vLLM 0.30.0)"; exit 1
    fi
  done
fi
EXTRA_VLLM_ARGS="${VLLM_ARGS:-}"
unset VLLM_PATCHES VLLM_ARGS  # vLLM warns about VLLM_* variables it does not know
MM=()
if grep -q '"vision_config"' "$MODEL/config.json" 2>/dev/null; then
  # vLLM's own cap is the one the shim sends with every image request: when the two differ, vLLM's first image request
  # is very slow
  CFG_PIXELS="$(python3 -c 'import json, sys; print((json.load(open(sys.argv[1])).get("image") or {}).get("max_pixels") or "")' "$HERE/serve_config.json" 2>/dev/null || true)"
  MAX_PIXELS="${MAX_PIXELS:-${CFG_PIXELS:-1605632}}"
  MM=(--limit-mm-per-prompt "{\"image\": ${MAX_IMAGES:-4}, \"video\": 0}"
      --mm-processor-kwargs "{\"max_pixels\": ${MAX_PIXELS}}")
fi
# the decisions read log-probabilities at temperature 0 and never sample, so
# FlashInfer's sampler (a JIT build that fails with an older system CUDA toolkit) is off unless
# VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER says otherwise
export VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER="${VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER:-0}"
SERVED=(--served-model-name h2oai/h2o-lightning-4b --host 127.0.0.1 --max-model-len "${MAX_LEN:-40960}")
gen_engine() {  # $1 = port: a stock generate-mode vLLM (one-way readouts, Box generation)
  vllm serve "$MODEL" "${SERVED[@]}" --port "$1" --gpu-memory-utilization "${GPU_UTIL:-0.90}" ${MM[@]+"${MM[@]}"} ${EXTRA_VLLM_ARGS}
}
wait_engine() {  # $1 = pid, $2 = port, $3 = name: until /health answers; a dead process is a failure (return 1)
  until curl -sf "http://127.0.0.1:$2/health" > /dev/null; do
    kill -0 "$1" 2>/dev/null || { echo "$3 exited"; return 1; }
    sleep 5
  done
}
PIDS=(); SHIM_ARGS=()
trap 'kill ${PIDS[@]+"${PIDS[@]}"} 2>/dev/null; rm -f -- "${SHIM_READY_FILE:-}"; wait' EXIT INT TERM
if [ "$ONE_ENGINE" = 1 ]; then
  vllm serve "$MODEL" "${SERVED[@]}" --port "$VLLM_PORT" --gpu-memory-utilization "${GPU_UTIL:-0.90}" \
    --attention-backend TRITON_ATTN ${MM[@]+"${MM[@]}"} ${EXTRA_VLLM_ARGS} --max-num-batched-tokens "${MAX_LEN:-40960}" & GEN_PID=$!; PIDS+=("$GEN_PID")
  # (the batch budget comes last: a two-way prompt is prefilled whole, so a smaller VLLM_ARGS value cannot override it)
  wait_engine "$GEN_PID" "$VLLM_PORT" "vLLM" || exit 1
  SHIM_ARGS+=(--vllm "http://127.0.0.1:$VLLM_PORT")
  export SHIM_TWO_WAY_FLAG=1
  if [ "$TWO_WAY" = 0 ]; then export SHIM_ATTENTION_MODE=causal; fi   # the one engine answering one-way by default
else
  gen_engine "$VLLM_PORT" & GEN_PID=$!; PIDS+=("$GEN_PID")
  SHIM_ARGS+=(--vllm "http://127.0.0.1:$VLLM_PORT")
fi
# /health answers 503 ("warming": true) until the warm-up below has passed; serve.sh itself waits on the pre-warm-up check
export SHIM_READY_FILE="$(mktemp -u)"
python3 "$HERE/h2o_lightning_shim.py" --config "$HERE/serve_config.json" --model-dir "$MODEL" --port "$PORT" "${SHIM_ARGS[@]}" &
SHIM_PID=$!; PIDS+=("$SHIM_PID")
until curl -sf "http://127.0.0.1:$PORT/health?live=1" > /dev/null; do
  for P in "${PIDS[@]}"; do kill -0 "$P" 2>/dev/null || { echo "a server exited"; exit 1; }; done
  sleep 5
done
# warm-up through the shim, so no real request pays a first-use cost (CUDA graphs, compile, the shim's two-way start-up
# check, the image processor): every path the card uses, in each mode this deployment serves (two-way and one-way on the
# one engine; one-way alone with TWO_WAY=0): a short yes/no question, a three-question request (choice, yes/no, score),
# a long record (about a sixth of MAX_LEN, capped at 30,000 tokens), and a small image when the model reads images (Box,
# off by default, is not warmed). A failed text warm-up stops the
# script; a failed image warm-up is a warning. Only then does /health turn ready and the ready line print.
warm() {  # $1 = a request body (sent on stdin: a long record exceeds the command-line limit as an argument)
  printf '%s' "$1" | curl -sf -m 900 "http://127.0.0.1:$PORT/v1/systemone" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' --data-binary @- > /dev/null
}
N=$(( ${MAX_LEN:-40960} / 24 )); [ "$N" -gt 7500 ] && N=7500     # 4 tokens per repeat: about MAX_LEN/6, at most 30,000
LONG="$(printf 'warm-up record line %.0s' $(seq 1 "$N"))"
MODES=(""); [ "$ONE_ENGINE" = 1 ] && MODES=('"attention_mode": "bidirectional", ' '"attention_mode": "causal", ')
for M in "${MODES[@]}"; do
  warm "{${M}\"state\": \"warm-up\", \"questions\": {\"d\": {\"type\": \"noul\", \"instructions\": \"Is this a warm-up?\",
    \"criteria\": {\"true\": \"yes\", \"false\": \"no\"}}}}" || { echo "the warm-up request failed (${M:-default mode}; see the shim's lines above)"; exit 1; }
  warm "{${M}\"state\": {\"ticket\": \"warm-up\"}, \"questions\": {
    \"c\": {\"type\": \"choice\", \"instructions\": \"Which team?\", \"criteria\": {\"a\": \"billing\", \"b\": \"support\", \"c\": \"sales\"}},
    \"n\": {\"type\": \"noul\", \"instructions\": \"Is it urgent?\", \"criteria\": {\"true\": \"yes\", \"false\": \"no\"}},
    \"s\": {\"type\": \"score\", \"instructions\": \"How urgent?\", \"criteria\": [\"low\", \"medium\", \"high\"]}}}" \
    || { echo "the three-question warm-up failed (${M:-default mode})"; exit 1; }
  warm "{${M}\"state\": \"$LONG\", \"questions\": {\"d\": {\"type\": \"choice\", \"instructions\": \"Which?\",
    \"criteria\": {\"a\": \"first\", \"b\": \"second\"}}}}" || { echo "the long-record warm-up failed (${M:-default mode})"; exit 1; }
  if [ ${#MM[@]} -gt 0 ]; then
    warm "{${M}\"state\": {\"images\": [\"data:image/png;base64,iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAEAAAABACAIAAAAlC+aJAAAAT0lEQVR42u3PQQ0AAAgEILV/sItlCh9u0IBOUp9NPScgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICBwbwFY4wLYRBP0hgAAAABJRU5ErkJggg==\"], \"note\": \"warm-up\"},
      \"questions\": {\"d\": {\"type\": \"noul\", \"instructions\": \"Is this a warm-up?\", \"criteria\": {\"true\": \"yes\", \"false\": \"no\"}}}}" \
      || echo "warning: the image warm-up request failed (${M:-default mode}); serving anyway" >&2
  fi
done
: > "$SHIM_READY_FILE"
curl -sf "http://127.0.0.1:$PORT/health" > /dev/null || { echo "the shim did not turn ready after the warm-up"; exit 1; }
if [ "$ONE_ENGINE" = 1 ]; then
  echo "H2O-Lightning-4B ready on http://127.0.0.1:$PORT/v1/systemone (one engine: two-way by default, one-way per request; Box $([ "$BOX" = 1 ] && echo on || echo off))"
else
  echo "H2O-Lightning-4B ready on http://127.0.0.1:$PORT/v1/systemone (one-way attention)"
fi
wait
